## Meet Dr. Ricciardi, Dr. Silvaroli & Dr. Lim

Our team is comprised of three dental experts dedicated to comprehensive smile restoration. Dr. Ricciardi, a Diplomate of the American Board of Oral Implantology and Implant Dentistry, provides advanced implant surgery expertise, Dr. Silvaroli delivers skilled restorative care focused on function and aesthetics, and Dr. Lim, a prosthodontist, specializes in the precise restoration and replacement of teeth. Together, they’re able to restore and reconstruct smiles [near Carson City](dentist-near-carson-city.html) to enhance their function and beauty.

[Meet Dr. Ricciardi](meet-dr-ricciardi.html) [Meet Dr. Silvaroli](meet-dr-silvaroli.html) [Meet Dr. Lim](meet-dr-lim.html)

 ![Collage of dental treatment chairs and front desk at Smile Design Implant Centers](assets/images/index-tech-mob.jpg)

## Ideal Results Using  
the Latest Technology

Our office is equipped with the latest dental technology, including our cone beam CT scanner that can capture a 3D digital blueprint of the entire facial anatomy, and our putty-free digital impression system.

[Tour the Office](tour-our-office.html)

for a Healthier Smile

Implants

With the ability to restore both the roots and the crowns of teeth, dental implants offer a variety of life-changing benefits for patients experiencing tooth loss, like unmatched stability.

## Partnering with  
Renown Dental Labs

Our specialists work with artisan dental laboratories to provide our patients with the best results possible in terms of both lifespan and aesthetics. We also have our own talented in-house lab technician, Mariana, that creates our 3D printer oral appliances, wax-ups, and surgical guides in-house.

## Frequently Asked Questions

Want to learn more about dental implants or any of the other services we offer? Our team is ready to answer your questions! Here are our responses to a few particularly common inquiries.

### What is the Best Way to Find a Dentist Who Does Dental Implants?

First, it’s important to remember that not all dental practices handle dental implants the same way. The process can be divided into two main stages: placement and restoration. Some dentists are able to handle both under one roof, but others might have to refer you to an expert for placement. It’s important to fully understand what approach a given practice will take before you make any commitments.

It’s also worth noting that specific types of dentists specialize in dental implants. In particular, prosthodontists such as Dr. Silvaroli and Dr. Lim are trained to handle both placement and restoration. As such, choosing a prosthodontist in Reno for your dental implant procedure can help ensure that you’re able to enjoy truly excellent results.

### How Can I Make a Same-Day Appointment with a Dentist?

Dental emergencies tend to strike when you least expect them. If you’re ever in a situation where you need to see a dentist right away (such as if you are experiencing severe tooth pain), we recommend that you call our office immediately. One of our team members will set up an appointment for you while also providing you with any first-aid advice you need.

The main goal of a same-day appointment is to figure out what’s going on inside your mouth so that we can create a treatment plan. Also, if you’re currently experiencing any pain, we’ll take whatever steps we can to help you find relief.

### How Do I Find the Best Place to Get Dental Work Done?

While you can always perform a Google search for “dentist near me” or “prosthodontist near me”, finding a practice that’s truly right for you requires careful consideration of your own unique needs. Write down the traits that matter most to you (such as “accepts my dental insurance” or “is located nearby”) so that you can look for a practice that meets them.

When you find a dentist or a prosthodontist who seems to be a good match for you, check to see if they’ve received any reviews on Google or Facebook. This can give you an idea of what you can expect if you schedule an appointment with them.

### How Much Does It Cost to Get a Tooth Pulled Without Insurance?

In the United States, having a tooth removed often costs between $150 and $400. However, our team can only give you a reliable estimate once we’ve examined your mouth and learned more about your needs. This is because the cost of a tooth extraction can vary significantly from patient to patient due to a number of factors, such as the difficulty of the procedure and the number of teeth involved.

# Contact Our Reno Dental Office

## We’re Looking Forward to Hearing from You!

If you have questions or concerns about your dental wellness, or you’re ready to schedule an appointment to begin getting your smile back on track, we look forward to hearing from you! Simply call our dental office in Reno using the number listed below or send us an email. We’re open bright and early four days a week, and also accept same-day emergency patients. When you call, one of our front desk staff will answer and be able to provide you with all the information you need. Above all else, we want you to feel confident about the dental care you’ll receive here, so we’ll do what we can to ensure a smooth and patient-focused experience from start to finish!

# Request a Dental Appointment Reno

## Schedule an Appointment That Works for You

Requesting an appointment with our dental practice in Reno has never been easier! Simply fill out the form linked below, and you’ll be able to submit it digitally. It’ll cover some basic information, including your name, the reason for your appointment, and the best way of contacting us. You’ll hear back from one of our Smile Design Implant Centers team members within 72 hours of submitting this request, and we’ll finalize the details of your visit with you and provide any other useful information.

# Refer a Dental Patient Reno

## Proudly Accepting New Patient Referrals

Do you have a friend or family member in need of life-changing dental care? Please send them our way! Our team at Smile Design Implant Centers is thrilled to accept dental patient referrals here in Reno; we understand that finding a reliable dental office isn’t always easy, so please allow us to help. We also proudly accept referrals from other dentists. If you’re a new patient, please let us know how you found out about us; we can’t wait to meet you!

To learn more about our office and see example treatments, check out our [newsletter](<https:// https://samsgroup.lpages.co/tfo-reno-smile-design/>).

# New Dental Patient Information Reno

## We’d Like to Welcome You to Our Reno Dental Office

Is your first appointment here at Smile Design Implant Centers rapidly approaching? We are very excited to meet you! To make sure that things go well on the big day, we ask that you read through the general information listed below in advance of your visit. We know that seeing a dentist for the first time can be a little daunting, so please feel free to let us know beforehand if you have any questions or specific accommodations.

## Your First Visit

![Smiling man in dental chair shaking hands with his Reno dentist](assets/images/for-patients-1.webp)

We know that first impressions matter in today’s world; so that’s why we’ll work hard to make sure your first visit is positive, rewarding, and has you wanting to return!

Your first consultation should take about an hour; this includes you arriving at our office, coming back to an operatory for your oral evaluation, scans, and X-rays, and of course, chatting with our team and carefully reviewing all possible avenues of treatment that might benefit your smile. We’ll also spend some time getting to know you and your smile goals; gauging this really helps us deliver results you’ll love.

However, none of this will feel rushed at all; both Dr. Ricciardi, Dr. Silvaroli, and Dr. Lim know that a healthy, beautiful smile isn’t something that can be achieved overnight; it takes careful consideration and commitment from all parties involved. That said, this first visit is a positive step in that direction!

## Dental Patient Forms

![Person filling out paperwork](assets/images/for-patients-2.webp)

We love getting new patients at Smile Design Implant Centers! If you’re visiting our office for the first time, we ask that you fill out the online patient forms we have below. Please fill these out thoroughly and completely so we can provide you with the most accurate care possible. Of course, if you need to wait until you’ve arrived at our office to complete them, that’s fine too. We look forward to meeting you!

[Online Patient Forms](https://forms.wv3.io/form?packetId=fdcac795-efe4-49ad-9f84-6045436ae682&companyId=31172a3b-944c-47d1-8a98-42ce68f4644f)

## Dental Insurance

![Person typing on calculator with one hand and filling out paperwork with the other](assets/images/for-patients-3.webp)

Note that our office is not in-network with any insurance providers. However, our team is more than happy to work with patients who have dental insurance. We can assist with the filing and billing parts of the process, and if you have questions about the specifics of your coverage, we’re more than happy to provide clarification. Remember, dental insurance is there to help you affordably maintain your oral health; so don’t hesitate to use it to serve your smile!

## Financing Options

![Young woman smiling while using her laptop](assets/images/for-patients-4.webp)

Lacking dental insurance should never be something that prevents your smile from receiving the care it needs to stay healthy and bright. That’s why we’re proud partners with CareCredit, a third-party financer that allows qualifying patients to break up their cost of dental care into manageable installments with low (or no) interest rates. In addition to CareCredit, we also work with Proceed Finance and iCreditworks, which function similarly. Feel free to call our office to learn more about these options and how to sign up.

[Apply for CareCredit](https://www.carecredit.com/doctor-locator/general-dentist-in-reno-nv-89521/smile-design-implant-centers/)

[Apply for Proceed Finance](https://wv3.io/6ak775Ao)

[iCreditworks](https://www.icreditworks.com/)

[![Smile Design Implant Centers Creating Confidence with Every Smile](assets/images/logo-footer.png)](/)

# About Our Reno Dental Office

## Restore Your Smile with Confidence & Ease

At Smile Design Implant Centers, we’re experts at restoring smiles. As dental specialists, Dr. Ricciardi, Dr. Silvaroli, and Dr. Lim have completed advanced training both academically and clinically to provide the best results in the treatment of tooth loss and dental damage. Experience not only outstanding results when you come to see us, but also the warm and comfortable atmosphere created by our 5-star team. To learn more about what makes our Reno dental office so unique, read below. Be sure to schedule your appointment today!

## Complimentary Consultations

![Woman in dental chair smiling at her Reno dentist](assets/images/about-1.webp)

Confident that our top-tier service and extensive smile gallery will wow you, we offer complimentary consultations for all of our patients wanting to take the jump towards restoring their smiles. This pressure-free visit consists of a one-on-one comprehensive evaluation with one of our specialists. They will help you explore the different services that could improve the function, appearance, and health of your smile based on your goals. Call us today to schedule your 100% complimentary consultation!

## Talented Team of Specialists

![Team of oral surgeons treating a patient](assets/images/about-2.webp)

Our dental team isn’t just comprised of general dentists. Born and raised in Reno, Dr. Ricciardi attended the University of Nevada, Reno, and is a skilled dentist as well as a Diplomate of the American Board of Oral Implantology and Implant Dentistry, a distinction that only about 500 dentists internationally have been designated. He’s also the _only_ dentist in Northern Nevada with this accreditation.

## High-Quality Prosthetic Options

![Dental professional creating a denture](assets/images/about-3.webp)

When it comes to restoring smiles, we’re careful to partner with dental laboratories that we trust to create the highest quality replacement teeth. For dentures and removable prosthetics, we utilize Tip Top Dental Lab, a local team of experts out of Reno. For our more complex cases, like for implant-retained prosthetics, we use Natural Aesthetics in Utah and Do Aesthetics in California. In addition to outside labs, we also have an in-house lab technician, Mariana, who crafts 3D printed nightguards, wax-ups, and surgical guides under our very own roof.

## Advanced Dental Technology

![Dentist showing a patient a model of their teeth on a screen](assets/images/about-4.webp)

Promising phenomenal results is one thing, but in order to deliver on those promises, it’s crucial to have a facility that’s equipped with all of the latest and most precise dental technology available. Our dental practice is home to a state-of-the-art 3D printer, cone beam CT scanner, digital impression system, intraoral cameras, and so much more. Each of these devices allows us to make sure that every detail of your treatment plan is thoroughly thought-out so you can enjoy long-lasting, time-tested results.

# Our Dental Mission Reno

## Promoting Lifelong Dental Wellness

Smile Design Implant Centers were founded by Dr. Steve Lim and Dr. Joseph Field with the simple mission of improving the lives of patients who seek to live healthier and happier lives. Their passion is to give patients a confident smile through dental implants. Dr. Lim is a Board-Certified Prosthodontist who received his dental and postgraduate degrees at Tufts School of Dental Medicine. Dr. Field received his dental degree from USC and is a Diplomate of the American Board of Oral Implantology. Dr. Lim and Dr. Field have been partners and friends for many years and have residences near Lake Tahoe.

There are 36 million Americans who suffer from edentulism (missing all of their teeth). 90% of these patients utilize removable dentures to compensate for their missing teeth. Edentulism affects our most vulnerable populations - the aging and the economically disadvantaged. Consequences of missing teeth include significant nutritional changes, diabetes, coronary artery disease, and some forms of cancer. Our dentist in Reno aims to provide patients who suffer from edentulism an avenue to regain their normal quality of life by providing a fixed appliance that utilizes dental implants and Smile Lock technology.

Drs. Lim and Field selected Reno as a great location due to the demand and need for transformational dentistry. They researched how many removable denture labs are located in the greater Reno area and the demographics of the number of patients using removable prostheses.

Having practiced dentistry for a combined 40 years, Dr. Lim and Dr. Field’s passion is in the art and science of replacing missing teeth. They both feel that the most rewarding experience in dentistry is being able to transform someone’s life by giving them the ability to chew and function as if they had their own teeth.

Dr. Lim and Dr. Field both enjoy the beauty of the outdoors, golfing and spending quality time with their family and friends. They are excited to meet you and look forward to exceeding your expectations.

# Meet the Dentists Reno

## Quality Care from Experienced, Friendly Professionals

When it comes to dentistry, Dr. Ricciardi, Dr. Silvaroli, and Dr. Lim share a common goal: to take a comprehensive approach and find meaningful ways to improve each patient’s smile in terms of appearance, comfort, and function. With Dr. Ricciardi’s advanced expertise in implant dentistry, Dr. Silvaroli’s focus on restorative care, and Dr. Lim’s specialized training as a prosthodontist, the level of complete and thorough care the practice provides is truly exceptional. Whether your smile needs a minor repair or a full transformation, you can feel confident that our Reno dental team will find a solution tailored to you and your lifestyle.

# Dentist Reno

## Get to Know Dr. Ricciardi

## About Dr. Ricciardi

![Reno oral surgeon Doctor Ricciardi](assets/images/dr-riccardi.jpg) [](#ricciardi)

![youtube thumbnail](assets/images/ricciardi-default-1.jpg)

Dr. Ricciardi is one of 500+ dentists in the United States, Canada, and internationally, who has been designated as a Diplomate of the [American Board of Oral Implantology/Implant Dentistry](http://www.aboi.org/) (ABOI/ID). He is the only dentist in Northern Nevada with this accreditation and one of only three in the state of Nevada.

The ABOI/ID Diplomate designation symbolizes the highest level of competence in implant dentistry. Certification by the ABOI/ID attests to the fact that a dentist has demonstrated knowledge, ability, and proficiency in implant dentistry through a rigorous examination process.

To sit for the examination, Dr. Ricciardi was required to practice implant dentistry for at least seven years and prove his dental implant expertise.

To earn the title of Diplomate, Dr. Ricciardi presented cases for review by an expert panel of implant dentists and passed a series of written and oral examinations sponsored by the ABOI/ID.

The ABOI/ID’s examination process measures the in-depth knowledge, proficient skills, and abilities required for practitioners to provide comprehensive, safe, and effective oral implant care for the public.

The ABOI/ID was founded in 1969 and conducts examinations to determine the qualifications and competence of dentists who voluntarily apply to the Board for certification.

## Who Is Dr. Ricciardi?

![Close up of golf club hitting a golf ball](assets/images/meet-dr-ricciardi-2.webp)

Dr. Ricciardi is a dentist who was born and raised in Reno, NV. He completed his undergraduate studies at the University of Nevada, Reno, from 1997-2001, where he studied biology and chemistry. He then obtained his doctor of dental surgery from Creighton University School of Dentistry in 2005. Following dental school, Dr. Ricciardi completed a general practice residency through the University of Nevada School of Medicine in 2006, focusing on IV conscious sedation, treatment of medically compromised patients, implant surgery, and other oral surgical procedures.

In 2014, he completed extensive surgical and prosthetic implant training in the Maxi course through the American Academy of Implant Dentistry. In 2016, Dr. Ricciardi received his associate fellowship in the AAID after passing extensive written and oral examinations. He was asked to join the Advanced Dental Implant Studies group in 2017 in Atlanta Georgia and was mentored by Dr. Edward Mills.

Dr. Ricciardi is a member of the American Dental Association, Nevada and Northern Nevada Dental Association, Advanced Dental Implant Studies group, and the American Dental Society of Anesthesiology. He is a fellow in the Academy of General Dentistry, and a fellow of the American Academy of Implant Dentistry, and a fellow of the Pierre Fauchard Academy. He has taken over 2,000 hours of implant-related continuing education courses.

He has volunteered with the Adopt a Vet program, Give Kids a Smile campaign, and the Northern Nevada Dental Health program. “Dentistry was always interesting to me because it combined artistry, medicine, and helping people obtain a better quality of life.”

In his spare time, Dr. Ricciardi likes to spend time with his wife and two kids, golf, snowboard, hunt, and fish. Fun fact: If pursuing academics or becoming a professional was not in the cards for him, acting or doing comedy would have been his dream career.

# Dentist Reno

## Get to Know Dr. Patrick Silvaroli

## About Dr. Silvaroli

![Reno prosthodontist Doctor Patrick Silvaroli](assets/images/meet-dr-silvaroli-1.webp)

Dr. Patrick Silvaroli prides himself on delivering exceptional, patient-centered care and has been serving his community in Northern Nevada since 2011. He continuously strives to not only deliver an outstanding level of dentistry but an experience that is personalized and best suited to each of his patient’s needs. He is proud to serve his country and is currently a member of the Nevada Air National Guard.

A native of Buffalo, NY, Dr. Patrick Silvaroli relocated with his family to Northern Nevada during his high school years. He is a graduate of Galena High School and received his Bachelor of Science in Biology with a Minor in Chemistry from the University of Nevada, Reno. He attended the University of Nevada, Las Vegas School of Dental Medicine, where he received his Doctor of Medicine in Dentistry (D.M.D.) in 2010. After dental school, Dr. Silvaroli completed a hospital-based General Practice Residency at Denver Health Regional Medical Center in Denver, Colorado.

It was here where he received advanced training in oral surgery, endodontics, pediatrics, and restorative dentistry, as well as the management of medically complex patients in both ambulatory and inpatient settings. He did his best to split his time between the hospital and snowboarding in the Rockies.

## Who is Dr. Silvaroli

![Rows of dental treatment stations with dummies for practice](assets/images/meet-dr-silvaroli-2.webp)

Dr. Silvaroli takes pride in continuing his education and was recently awarded the prestigious credential of Associate Fellow through the American Academy of Implant Dentistry (AFAAID). This is a rigorous, advanced dental implant training program that involves over 300 hours of didactic and clinical learning as well as successful completion of both a written and oral examination in front of an expert panel. Very few dentists receive this honor nationwide. Dr. Silvaroli’s academic and professional memberships include:

-   Academy of General Dentistry
-   American Academy of Implant Dentistry
-   American Academy of Cosmetic Dentistry
-   Sierra Society for Dental Excellence
-   Spear Study Club
-   Northern Nevada Dental Society
-   Nevada Dental Association
-   American Dental Association>

Dr. Silvaroli is also dedicated to serving both his colleagues and the community as a leader, an advocate, and a voice for the profession of dentistry. He currently serves on the executive committee with the Nevada Dental Association and is a board member with the Adopt-A-Vet Dental Program which provides pro bono dental care to our nation’s veterans. He is a member of The Reno Rodeo Association, The Reno Elks Lodge, and is a mentor for pre-dental students at the University of Nevada, Reno. Dr. Silvaroli was also awarded the Reno-Tahoe Twenty Under 40 Award which honors exceptional young leaders in the community.

## Outside of the Dental Office, What Do You Like to Do?

![Spinning object on fire](assets/images/meet-dr-silvaroli-3.webp)

When not practicing dentistry, Dr. Silvaroli loves spending time with his college sweetheart and wife Meesha, their daughter Kinsley, and their two bulldogs Presley and Cannoli. He enjoys working out, playing hockey, snowboarding in Tahoe and is a constant student of the guitar and piano. He also loves cooking Italian food and watching his beloved Buffalo Bills and Buffalo Sabres.

# Prosthodontist Reno

## Meet Dr. Steve Lim

## About Dr. Lim

![Reno oral surgeon Doctor Ricciardi](assets/images/meet-dr-lim-1.jpg)

Dr. Steve Lim is driven by one simple goal: helping patients feel healthier, more confident, and proud of their smiles. His commitment to excellence led him to complete advanced specialty training in prosthodontics and achieve board certification, a distinction that reflects the highest level of expertise in complex restorative and implant dentistry. With more than 25 years of experience, Dr. Lim has helped patients with needs ranging from straightforward restorations to full-mouth rehabilitation regain comfort, function, and lasting confidence.

## Where Did You Study Dentistry??

![Close up of golf club hitting a golf ball](assets/images/meet-dr-lim-2.jpg)

Dr. Lim earned his Bachelor of Science degree from the University of Washington before completing his Doctor of Dental Medicine degree at the Tufts University School of Dental Medicine. He then remained at Tufts to complete his postgraduate specialty training in prosthodontics. Today, he is a Diplomate of the American Board of Prosthodontics and maintains active involvement in leading professional organizations, reflecting his dedication to continued growth and clinical excellence.

In addition to patient care, Dr. Lim is deeply engaged in advancing the field of dentistry. He is the Founder and CEO of APS Global Ventures, a company focused on CAD/CAM implant component manufacturing, and he leads professional study clubs in both San Francisco and Silicon Valley. He regularly pursues continuing education, including advanced training through Spear Education, to ensure his patients benefit from the latest techniques and technology.

## Outside of the Dental Office, What Do You Like to Do?

![Close up of golf club hitting a golf ball](assets/images/meet-dr-lim-3.jpg)

Outside the office, Dr. Lim enjoys an active and creative life. A former Washington state champion wrestler, he now spends his free time skiing, golfing, playing piano and guitar, and making memories outdoors with his wife—an orthodontist—and their two sons.

# Meet Our Dental Team Reno

## Your Passionate New Partners in Dental Health

Here at Smile Design Implant Centers, we believe that our patients deserve the absolute best dental care possible. That’s why Dr. Ricciardi, Dr. Silvaroli, Dr. Lim, and our superb dental team in Reno have spent the last several years giving it their all, providing life-changing care and fostering beautiful smiles for patients with all sorts of varying needs. You’ll feel right at home and like a member of our family when you’re here, and we’ll certainly make sure you leave with a big smile on your face! To learn more about us and what we do, feel free to continue reading or contact our dental office; we can’t wait to hear from you.

## Mariana, Dental Lab Technician

![Dental Lab Tech Mariana](assets/images/team-mariana.jpg)

Mariana is our in-house dental lab technician with over four years of restorative dental lab experience. She enjoys working with an amazing team and watching patients’ confidence transform throughout their treatment journey. Mariana attended Western Nevada College and Truckee Meadows Community College. She is CPR-certified and highly experienced in printing dental models, nightguards, and surgical guides.

Outside of work, she loves traveling and going to concerts to listen to live music!

## Adriana, Dental Assistant/Back Office Manager

![Dental assistant Adriana](assets/images/team-adriana.jpg)

Adriana has well over a decade of experience in the dental field and runs our back office. She values being able to work with such a skilled, amazing team and enjoys being able to see the real-life impact of her work on patients’ lives. Adriana attended Milan Institute and is certified in coronal polishing, radiographs, and sterilizations. She also has experience with general dentistry, implant surgeries, and IV sedation.

Whenever she isn’t working, Adriana enjoys spending time with her husband of over 25 years, three children, and grandchildren. She also enjoys camping, side-by-side riding, family get-togethers, and taking care of her grandchildren.

## Melina, Dental Assistant

![Dental Assitant Melina](assets/images/team-melina.jpg)

Melina has been a dental assistant for over a decade and loves working with such an amazing team to help patients regain their confidence in themselves. She’s originally from Reno and graduated from the Milan Institute. Over her years of experience, she has gained clinical expertise in assisting with endodontic treatment, PRF, oral surgery, and IV sedation. She’s also certified in radiographs, coronal polishing, and CPR.

Melina has been married for over a decade and has one daughter and a dog. She enjoys spending time with her family and traveling whenever she isn’t in the office.

## Stephanie, Practice Manager

![Dental Assitant Melina](assets/images/team-temp.jpg)

Stephanie is originally from California's East Bay Area and made Reno her home in 2020. With nearly two decades of experience in dentistry, she has built her career working across multi-specialty practices, regional operations, practice consulting, and helping growing organizations create exceptional patient experiences.

As Practice Manager at Smile Design Implant Centers, Stephanie is passionate about making every patient's journey as comfortable and seamless as possible. She believes that restoring someone's smile is about more than teeth—it's about restoring confidence, improving quality of life, and helping people feel like themselves again. She enjoys supporting both patients and her team to ensure every visit reflects the high standard of care the practice is known for.

# Tour Our Reno Dental Office

## Your New Home for Quality Dental Care

We understand that not everyone is enthusiastic about visiting the dentist, which is why we’ve tried to make our office a space where you can walk in and feel both calm and confident about the care your smile is about to receive. Our Reno dental office is quiet, meticulously clean, and features all sorts of patient-friendly amenities, and from our waiting room to our operatories, you’ll never once feel uneasy. Feel free to take a closer look at our dental practice by browsing through some of our photographs linked below. If you like what you see, give us a call so we can schedule your appointment!

# Advanced Dental Technology Reno

## Using Technology to Precisely Reshape Smiles

Here at Smile Design Implant Centers, we know that technology can be quite an important asset in the world of modern dentistry. That’s why we’ve spared no expenses when it comes to outfitting our dental office with some of the best equipment available. To learn a little more about some of the advanced dental technology in Reno that we use to ensure optimal dental health for you and your loved ones, continue reading below or call us today.

## In-House 3D Printing

![Hand holding a 3 D printed denture made with advanced dental technology in Reno](assets/images/dental-tech-1.webp)

We’re excited to have our very own 3D printer here in our Reno dental office! Our team can use this technology to rapidly draw up and fabricate all kinds of different treatments and devices, including nightguards, surgical guides, wax-ups, and more. This ultimately saves everyone a great deal of time and money, since it expedites certain processes and makes it much simpler to deliver working results. Better yet, this machine ensures that absolutely nothing is sacrificed in terms of quality when it comes to these solutions.

## Digital Dental Impressions

![Dental patient having digital impressions of her teeth taken](assets/images/dental-tech-2.webp)

In the past, the process of taking dental impressions involved gooey, gag-inducing putty. We’re thrilled to eliminate this messy and cumbersome method and replace it with our digital impressions system. This technology offers a much more modern, efficient, and convenient way of taking incredibly precise impressions of your mouth, which can then be transferred to our dental laboratories to craft high-quality restorations and oral appliances.

## CT/Cone Beam Scanner

![Dental patient having a C T scan of her jaw taken](assets/images/dental-tech-3.webp)

Our cone beam CT scanner is effective for diagnosing and evaluating various types of dental conditions, as well as planning treatments. To put it simply, this modern tool makes it drastically easier for our team to look inside your mouth, see what issues are present, and begin putting together a game plan for getting things back on track! It essentially renders a 3D image reconstruction of your teeth, gums, tongue, throat, and the rest of your orofacial structures, which can then be viewed on a digital screen for comprehensive planning.

# Prosthodontics Reno

## Ensuring Your Smile Is Restored the Right Way

Research shows that around 25% of Americans say that their smile or teeth are the one facial feature they would like to change – and there’s perhaps no better professional to assist with this than a prosthodontist! Here at Smile Design Implant Centers, our Board-Certified prosthodontists in Reno, excels in understanding what a happy and healthy smile should look and feel like, and he’s ready to help you achieve that feat in a way that works for you. To learn more about our prosthodontic services or to schedule your appointment, please contact our dental office today.

## Why Choose Smile Design Implant Centers for Prosthodontics?

-   Board-Certified Prosthodontist on Staff
-   Partnered with Renown Dental Laboratories
-   Conveniently Located Dental Office

## What Is a Prosthodontist?

![Reno prosthodontist designing a denture](assets/images/prosthodontist-1.webp)

A prosthodontist is a dentist who specializes in the restoration and replacement of missing teeth. Our Reno prosthodontists have received additional training after dental school, and their expertise lies in restoring and replacing teeth. This allows him to treat complex cases that involve restoring damaged teeth or replacing missing teeth with custom restorations. Their training is focused on helping people restore their oral function and appearance, which often involves a combination of treatments such as crowns, bridges, onlays, veneers, dental implants, or a combination of these procedures. This makes our prosthodontists better prepared to restore your smile and help ensure your treatment is successful and long-lasting.

The field of cosmetic dentistry is constantly advancing, which requires prosthodontists to regularly attend continuing education courses to learn the latest techniques and materials. This ensures you receive the highest level of care from our prosthodontists while treating your case in the most predictable manner possible.

## Is Prosthodontics Right for Me?

![Close up of man smiling with several missing teeth](assets/images/prosthodontist-2.webp)

Yes, if you are missing one or more teeth and have healthy gums and enough bone in the jaw to support an implant, or if you’re looking for an expertly crafted removable denture or fixed traditional restoration. A consultation with a Board-Certified Reno prosthodontist is the best way to determine if you're a good candidate for dental implants, mini implants, or traditional prosthetics.

If you have gum disease, the disease will need to be treated before you undergo dental implant surgery. The disease causes pockets to develop around your teeth, which can turn into open spaces between your gums and teeth. These spaces can trap bacteria that cause further damage to your mouth and surrounding tissues. During your consultation with our Reno prosthodontist, they will examine your mouth to determine if you suffer from gum disease. If you do exhibit signs of periodontal disease, you will need to undergo periodontal therapy, which can include scaling and root planing or other treatment methods.

In addition to healthy gums and bone structure in your jaw, the ideal candidate also has a strong at-home oral care routine. Although you may be excited to complete your smile with dental implants, you must be committed to taking care of them every day to ensure they last for years to come. This means brushing twice daily with a soft-bristled toothbrush and flossing at least once a day. You may also need to use a special type of mouth rinse to prevent excessive bacterial growth around your dental implants.

# Dental Implants Reno

## The Gold Standard of Tooth Replacement

Dental implants are an incredible solution for replacing missing teeth because they can do things that other restorations can’t; for instance, they replace the root structure of missing teeth, whereas traditional dentures do not. But the advantages don’t stop there; dental implants offer all sorts of other impressive, life-changing benefits! To learn more about dental implants in Reno and how our team can use them to restore your smile and boost your quality of life, contact our office today.

## Why Choose Smile Design Implant Centers for Dental Implants?

-   Entire Implant Process Completed In-House
-   Flexible Financing Options Available
-   Skilled Dental Team with Years of Experience

![](assets/images/index-booklet.png)

## The Guide Will Show You

-   Dental Implant Options
-   Average Pricing
-   Factors that Affect Eligibillity
-   And More!

## What Are Dental Implants?

![Model of the mouth with a dental implant in Reno replacing a tooth](assets/images/dental-implants-1.webp)

Dental implants are small posts made from titanium that are surgically placed directly into a patient’s jawbone. Since they’re made from titanium, which is biocompatible with bone tissue, they can fuse with a patient’s jaw and act as replacement tooth roots for a corresponding dental restoration. They also offer incredible, unmatched oral functionality and effectively prevent future bone resorption. Better yet, under ideal circumstances, dental implants have the potential to last for decades or even a lifetime!

## The 4-Step Dental Implant Process

![Woman in dental chair grinning at her dentist](assets/images/dental-implants-2.webp)

Dental implants are placed through a specialized multi-step process that gives restorations mounted upon them the strength and stability they need to behave exactly like real teeth and last much longer than traditional tooth replacement devices. At Smile Design Implant Centers, our skilled team is qualified and equipped to carry out the entire treatment in-house, which helps make receiving dental implants a smooth and stress-free process. Here’s a quick look at how dental implant placement works, but please feel free to [contact our Reno office](https://www.smiledesignimplants.com/contact.html) if you’d like to know more or [schedule a consultation](https://www.smiledesignimplants.com/for-patients.html).

Read More

### Initial Dental Implant Consultation

![Lady schedules consultation over the phone](assets/images/1-4step-implants.jpg)

During this appointment, we’ll be able to evaluate your tooth loss and oral health to determine if dental implants are a viable option. Patients with insufficient jawbone density or compromised gum health might need to undergo preliminary procedures before receiving dental implants. Once you are approved for implants, our dentist will be happy to develop an effective treatment plan that suits your needs and fits your schedule.

### Dental Implant Surgery

![Dental hygienist smiles](assets/images/2-4step-implants.jpg)

We can complete the entire dental implant process here in our Reno dental office, which ultimately saves you time and energy and makes coordinating your schedule much easier. The procedure itself is very straightforward; our dentist places the implants where they’re needed directly into the jawbone via a minor surgery. This process is only performed after the patient receives an appropriate anesthetic to ensure their comfort, and most people who receive implants report that the procedure is much more comfortable than extractions.

### Dental Implant Osseointegration & Abutment

![Smile with dental implant](assets/images/3-4step-implants.jpg)

After the implants have been placed, the process of osseointegration begins, during which the biocompatible titanium posts fuse with the living jawbone tissue surrounding them. This establishes the sturdy foundation your implants need for maximum stability, longevity, and chewing power. After your implants fuse with your jaw over several months, you’ll come back to our office to have abutment pieces placed on the devices through another minor surgical procedure. We’ll also take detailed bite impressions during this appointment.

### Delivery of Dental Implant Restoration(s)

![Exploded illustration of dental bridge and dental implants](assets/images/4-4step-implants.jpg)

Once you have healed after receiving abutments (which usually takes about two weeks), you’ll return to our office to receive your custom-made dental restoration. After attaching it to your abutments and making sure that everything looks and functions correctly, we’ll happily send you on your way to enjoy your brand-new smile!

## Who Dental Implants Can Help

![Senior man holding coffee mug and looking out of a window](assets/images/dental-implants-3.webp)

If you’re interested in addressing tooth loss head-on with dental implants, we have good news for you: they are quite versatile! If you want to find out if you’re a candidate, then schedule a consultation with our Reno dental team so we can learn about both your dental needs and smile goals. If you want to learn more before booking an appointment, then read on.

Read More

### Who Is a Good Candidate for Dental Implants?

![Man smiling while sitting in treatment chair](assets/images/implant-help-1.jpg)

Simply put, there are a variety of factors that go into whether or not a patient is a good candidate for dental implants. A few examples include if you stay on top of your at-home and professional dental hygiene regimen, if you are willing to break bad habits, like smoking, and if your gums are healthy. Of course, dental implants require healthy bone structure to be successful, so patients should have enough natural bone to hold an implant as well. If your jawbone has receded over time, a bone graft may be required to correct this issue and allow you to receive dental implants.

### Missing One Tooth

![Illustration of single dental implant](assets/images/implant-help-2.jpg)

Whether there is a noticeable gap when you smile or you’re missing one of your back molars, don’t worry – dental implants may be an option! If that’s the case, we will insert the implant into your jawbone, wait for it to fuse properly, and then attach a custom dental crown on top. Not only will your replacement tooth look like the real thing, but it will feel equally as natural. So, you don’t have to worry about unwanted attention being drawn to your smile; the final result will be seamless.

### Missing Multiple Teeth

![Illustration of implant bridge](assets/images/implant-help-3.jpg)

Previously, dental bridges or partial dentures were required to replace several missing teeth. Although these are still great options, dental implants are considerably more popular because they are the only tooth-replacement solution that stimulates your jawbone (which helps prevent bone loss). Plus, your healthy enamel doesn’t need to be altered in the process of filling the gaps in your smile. So, if you are missing several teeth in a row or multiple teeth throughout your mouth, consider dental implants!

### Missing All Teeth

![Illustration of implant denture](assets/images/implant-help-4.jpg)

If you are missing all of your teeth, then you know first-hand how difficult it can be to smile confidently, eat a well-balanced diet, and communicate clearly. Fortunately, a denture can be attached to 4-8 strategically placed dental implants, giving you a healthy, complete, and beautiful smile again. If this sounds like the solution you’ve been looking for, then don’t hesitate to schedule a consultation at our Reno dental office so we can determine if this is the best tooth-replacement option for you. There’s a good chance the answer is “yes!”

## Benefits of Dental Implants

![Dentist showing sample dental implant to woman in dental chair](assets/images/1-benefits-dental-implants.jpg)

Many experts consider these replacement teeth the ideal way to rebuild smiles after tooth loss because of their unique advantages. Unlike dentures and bridges, they are embedded directly into your jaw and eventually fuse with the bone, which often makes them sturdier and more reliable than the alternatives.

Continue reading to learn more about the different benefits of getting dental implants and please feel free to contact us to [schedule an appointment.](https://www.smiledesignimplants.com/for-patients.html)

Read More

### Day-to-Day Benefits

![Nose-to-neck closeup of man pointing to a tooth indicated as an implant](assets/images/2-benefits-dental-implants.jpg)

These restorations are the only ones that are surgically placed into your jawbone so they function more like natural teeth. This provides several day-to-day advantages, including:

-   **Improved enunciation.** Gaps in your grin left behind by missing teeth can result in a lisp, slur, or other changes to your speech patterns, which can be overcome with new teeth in place.
-   **Better bite force.** This is the only treatment that can return up to 80% or more of your original chewing power, so there are fewer dietary restrictions.
-   **Boosted confidence.** Your implants are capped with a lifelike restoration customized to look and feel as natural as possible to enhance your self-esteem.

### Health Benefits

![Man in denim shirt smiling at dentist about to examine his mouth](assets/images/3-benefits-dental-implants.jpg)

Not only can these reliable replacements offer several daily advantages, but they can also potentially help improve your oral and overall health in the following ways:

-   **Easy maintenance.** Keeping your implants in good shape can be as easy as brushing and flossing twice daily and visiting our team every 6 months for a routine checkup and cleaning.
-   **Preserve your jawbone.** They are the only treatment that involves placing a support rod into your jaw, which functions like a root to keep it active and healthy, preventing the degeneration that typically accompanies tooth loss.
-   **Prevent dental drifting.** The remaining teeth in your mouth are prone to shifting into empty spaces, wearing down your enamel, and causing oral issues. Your implants help ensure everything remains in its rightful position.

### Long-Term Benefits

![Woman in dental chair smiling with dentist behind her touching her shoulders](assets/images/4-benefits-dental-implants.jpg)

Your new teeth also provide several long-term advantages, such as:

-   **High success rate.** The procedure has an incredibly high success rate of over 90%, meaning most patients won’t develop complications as long as they follow the post-operative instructions closely.
-   **Resilient materials.** They’re made from durable materials designed to withstand the pressure of biting and chewing, and can last 20+ years if cared for properly.
-   **Save money over time.** They’re less likely to become destabilized or dislodged, which often results in costly repairs or replacement. Plus, there’s no need to invest regularly in additional accessories and supplies, such as adhesives, cleaners, or soaking solutions.

## Understanding the Cost of Dental Implants

![Giant plastic tooth next to a piggy bank and dollar bills ](assets/images/implant-cost-1.jpg)

Implant restorations involve an advanced and complex treatment process, so understanding the cost of dental implants in Reno isn’t always straightforward. Rest assured, your implant dentist is dedicated to financial clarity and will discuss your potential expenses with you during a consultation. Our team is also happy to help you file any dental insurance claims or explore other [affordability options](https://www.smiledesignimplants.com/for-patients.html). 

Read More

### Preliminary Treatments & Dental Implant Surgery

![Close up of dental implants in a resin display case](assets/images/implant-cost-2.jpg)

**Preliminary treatments** include procedures like gum disease therapy and bone grafting. The purpose of including them in a dental implant plan is to restore your oral health or strengthen your jawbone in order to set you up for treatment success. In other words, not everyone needs preliminary treatment.

Since **dental implant surgery** is considered as a separate procedure, patients who do need preliminary treatments will have more overall expenses. However, they’ll also likely avoid costs related to dental implant failure. Rest assured, both your smile and your wallet will appreciate it if you follow your implant dentist’s recommendations!

### The Parts of Your Dental Implant

![Dentist holding a dental implant model and crown](assets/images/implant-cost-3.jpg)

A dental implant restoration involves three main parts: the titanium post anchored to the jawbone, the replacement teeth, and the abutment that connects them. The abutment is often included in the cost of the dental implant, which have fairly “standard” prices. The price of your replacement teeth, though, will vary depending on whether you need a single crown, bridge, or denture.

### How Dental Implants Can Save You Money

![Man holding a blue piggy bank](assets/images/implant-cost-4.jpg)

One of the advantages of a dental implant restoration is that they can last twice as long as their traditional counterparts. With proper care, for example, a traditional denture can hold up for about 15 years, while an implant denture can support your smile for 30 years or more. You’ll likely need fewer repairs and maintenance appointments with dental implants, saving you money in the long run.

### Does My Dental Insurance Cover Dental Implants?

![Dental insurance form sitting on a wood desk](assets/images/implant-cost-5.jpg)

Dental implants themselves, unfortunately, are usually considered to be a “luxury” expense by dental insurance plans. As such, few policies cover costs associated with the post or abutment.

The good news is that most dental insurance plans _do_ cover other aspects of a dental implant treatment, such as oral surgery, preliminary procedures, and replacement teeth. So your policy could still help you offset some of your expenses.

### Making Dental Implants Affordable

![Plan growing out of a jar full of coins](assets/images/implant-cost-6.jpg)

If you don’t have dental insurance, you may be interested in flexible financing options. Our office works with CareCredit, Proceed Finance, and iCreditworks – all reputable third-party financiers who offer little-to-no-interest monthly payment plans. If you’re approved, you’ll be able to pay at your own pace and spread your treatment costs out over multiple paychecks.

## Maintaining & Caring for Your Dental Implants

![Dentist smiling at patient in treatment chair](assets/images/1maintcareimp.jpg)

Can you imagine investing your valuable time and money in the dental implant process, only for yours to fail within a matter of months or years? Fortunately, there are several steps you can take to prevent that from happening (as well as a few habits you should avoid). To learn what they are, read on!

Read More

### Make Oral Hygiene a Priority

![Man smiling while brushing his teeth in bathroom](assets/images/2maintcareimp.jpg)

Although your dental implants are artificial and, therefore, cannot decay, your natural teeth still can, and your gums can develop an infection if you neglect them. So, make sure that you focus on being consistent with brushing, flossing, and rinsing with mouthwash. It’s also important to replace your toothbrush every quarter, clean your toothbrush holder, and throw out any expired products.

### Eat a Healthy Diet

![Woman smiling while eating healthy meal at home](assets/images/3maintcareimp.jpg)

While some foods benefit your oral health, others have the opposite effect. That’s why it’s so important to stick to a healthy, well-balanced diet that’s filled with lots of smile-friendly foods, like fresh fruits and lean proteins. It’s equally as important to steer clear of hard pretzels, candy canes, taffy, peanut brittle, and other foods that can tug on your restoration or break it.

### Break Bad Habits

![Closeup of patient biting her nails](assets/images/4maintcareimp.jpg)

Some habits are known for being dangerous, like smoking. Others seem harmless, like biting your nails or chewing on ice cubes. The reality is that even these increase your risk of dental implant failure. So, if you haven’t already, now is the time to commit to quitting. If you need some help, don’t hesitate to ask us about what has worked for other patients!

Important note: Do NOT use your teeth as tools! Always use scissors to remove plastic wrapping or a bottle cap opener to remove a bottle cap. It’s a small step that will make a dramatic difference when it comes to the lifespan of your dental implants.

### Protect Your Dental Implants

![Two friends high-fiving in workout class](assets/images/5maintcareimp.jpg)

Implementing a solid oral hygiene regimen at home and breaking bad habits will protect your dental implants, but there’s another important step you need to take: wearing a mouthguard when you’re active. Similarly, you should wear a nightguard if you grind or clench your teeth at night. That way, your restoration doesn’t chip or crack under the pressure.

### Schedule Regular Dental Checkups

![Woman smiling at dentist during exam](assets/images/6maintcareimp.jpg)

As we mentioned above, dental implants cannot decay. It is possible for them to become loose, however. With routine checkups, we can monitor their condition while also screening for oral cancer, tooth decay, gum disease, and other serious problems. Plus, the cleanings ensure plaque and tartar are removed before they wreak havoc on your oral health.

## Dental Implant FAQs

### How Long Do Dental Implants Last? 

If properly cared for, your dental implants can last a lifetime. The implant itself is permanently secured to your jawbone, however its lifespan greatly depends on how well you take care of your oral health.

To ensure that they last as long as possible, it is important that you brush and floss them just as you would your natural teeth and attend dental checkups regularly. Furthermore, do not use them for anything other than chewing food, and try to avoid anything that is too hard or sticky.

Finally, if you are a smoker, it is important to quit, as in addition to causing myriad health problems, smoking can also increase your odds of implant failure.

### Can I Take Dental Implants Out?

No, you cannot take your dental implants out because they are permanently fixed to the bone. If they need to be removed, your dentist must perform the procedure. The crown and abutment portion of your implants can be removed and replaced as needed.

There are also some types of implant dentures that can be removed for cleaning. Your dentist can explain your options for what type of implant is best for your mouth at your consultation appointment.

### Does Getting Dental Implants Hurt?

Your mouth will be numbed with local anesthesia prior to the start of your dental implant procedure, so you should not feel any pain. You may also be sedated during this time, which will lower your body’s ability to register pain.

Though your jawbone does not have very many nerve endings, you may still experience a little soreness for a few days after the procedure. This can be alleviated with the help of over the counter or prescription pain medications as directed. You can also apply cold compresses for 20 minutes at a time.

Should your discomfort worsen instead of improving after two or three days, contact your dentist as soon as possible.

### Do Dental Implants Decay?

Because your dental implants are not biological teeth, they cannot decay or get cavities, however any of your remaining natural teeth still can. Your gums can also still become infected, causing a condition known as peri-implantitis. This is one of the main causes of dental implant failure. Thus, maintaining proper oral hygiene is still extremely important after dental implant surgery, even if you’ve replaced all of your teeth.

## Dental Implant Post-Op Instructions

![Older man with glass smiling with new dental implants in Reno ](assets/images/imp-post-op-1.jpg)

Once you receive your new dental implants, there are a few steps you’ll need to take to ensure a swift and successful recovery. The dental implant post-op instructions in Reno that you’ll receive will help you avoid complications and instead encourage proper healing as you move one step closer to enjoying life with a new, permanent smile.

Read More

### What to Do Directly After Dental Implant Surgery

![Man asleep in bed (What to Do Directly After Dental Implant Surgery ](assets/images/imp-post-op-2.jpg)

When your dental implant surgery is complete, you’ll need to make sure that you have someone who can take you home to start the recovery process. The anesthesia administered at the start of your procedure will need time to wear off, so you will be unable to drive.

Once you are home, make sure to have an over-the-counter pain reliever readily accessible so that you can take it once you notice the anesthesia wearing off. This will reduce the amount of discomfort you experience, which is normal.

If you smoke, do not attempt to pick up your habit right away. Instead, wait at least 24 hours. Nicotine will only prolong the healing process – a factor that causes many dentists to strongly urge patients to quit before receiving dental implants.

### Common Side Effects

![Woman dealing with mouth pain ](assets/images/imp-post-op-3.jpg)

Do not be alarmed if you notice some slight bleeding and swelling after your procedure. This is normal. It will take time for your gums and bone to heal, so feel free to use a cold compress to reduce inflammation and discomfort. If you do notice that these post-operative side effects do not decrease but instead increase, call our office right away.

### Diet

![A warm bowl of soup ](assets/images/imp-post-op-4.jpg)

The first several days after your procedure, you’ll want to stick to a liquid diet to allow your mouth more time to heal. After that, you are free to incorporate soft foods such as pudding, yogurt, mashed potatoes, etc. This will allow you to receive more nutrients but avoid pressure placed on your gums and bone.

Once your mouth begins to feel “normal,” you can introduce solid foods into your diet.

### Health & Oral Hygiene

![Young woman brushing her teeth](assets/images/imp-post-op-5.jpg)

The day you return home from your procedure, you’ll need to leave your mouth untouched; however, after a full 24 hours, you can resume your normal oral hygiene routine. Brushing and flossing are essential to ensure bad bacteria do not infect the surgical sites, but you will need to be careful not to aggravate your dental implants in Reno.

Instead of using mouthwash to rinse your mouth, gargle with warm salt water, as this will help to alleviate discomfort and encourage healing. Just be careful not to spit.

### What to Do After Your New Teeth Are Attached

![Older man in leather chair smiling with dental implants ](assets/images/imp-post-op-6.jpg)

Once your custom restoration is secured in place by your implant dentist in Reno, you can begin to enjoy life with your new dental implants. It’s not uncommon to experience some slight sensitivity; however, this will dissipate within a few days. Also, you may need time to adjust to your new teeth, especially a denture, if you’ve been without teeth for some time.

# Implant Dentures Reno

## An Exciting and Advanced Method of Tooth Replacement

At Smile Design Implant Centers, an implant denture is a dental implant designed to replace a missing tooth. In addition to replacing the tooth, the dental implant will also hold your denture in place.

Denture retention implants are secured into the bone underneath your denture. The implant has minor grooves that line up with your denture, allowing the denture to snap into place.

## Types of Implant Dentures

![Illustrated full implant denture in Reno on lower arch](assets/images/implant-overdentures-1.webp)

There are two types of implant dentures in Reno: ball-retained and bar-retained. A ball-retained implant denture has a ball-shaped attachment that snaps on and off the implant. A bar-retained implant denture has a bar-shaped attachment that clips onto the implant. Both types are easy to remove for cleaning.

## Implant Dentures vs. Traditional Dentures

![Gloved hand holding a full denture](assets/images/implant-overdentures-2.webp)

Traditional dentures are removable and function primarily as replacements for missing teeth. They rely on suction or adhesive to stay securely in place.

Traditional dentures can be uncomfortable, and patients often find that they move around. They also do not feel natural. Many patients also struggle with salivation, and a lisp may develop over time.

On the other hand, implant dentures are securely fixed, so they always stay in place. They are also customized to look and feel natural and do not interfere with your speech.

## How Are Implant Dentures Placed?

![Oral surgeon and assistant performing dental implant surgery](assets/images/implant-overdentures-3.webp)

Our oral surgeon will carefully place the implant into the jawbone. This procedure is commonly performed in the office under local anesthesia. Once they place the implant, it will need to heal. The exact amount of time varies based on the patient’s overall health, but it can take anywhere from three to six months to heal. During this time, the jawbone will fuse with the implant, creating a stable base for the denture.

Our dentist will monitor the healing to ensure the implant has successfully fused with the jawbone. They can also take impressions of the patient’s teeth to design a denture that fits into the implant. They will send the mold to a dental lab, where your denture will be created. Once the final denture is ready, our dentist will secure the denture to the implant.

## The Benefits of Implant Dentures

![Smiling senior woman touching her face](assets/images/implant-overdentures-4.webp)

Implant overdentures are a permanent solution to tooth loss. In addition to providing full chewing function, these implants also prevent bone loss in the jaw. Traditional dentures do not prevent bone loss, making them a poor long-term solution for tooth loss. Implant dentures are superior to conventional dentures because they are both functional and aesthetically pleasing.

# All-On-X Dental Implants Reno

## Giving Your Smile a New Lease on Life

All-On-X is a dental implant procedure designed for patients who have lost all of their upper or lower teeth. All-On-X dental implants in Reno are secured into your mouth with a fixed denture that replaces all of your teeth at one time.

## What Is All-On-X Dental Implant Treatment?

![Illustrated All on X denture being placed onto four dental implants](assets/images/all-on-x-dental-implants-1.webp)

An All-On-X device is a solution for patients looking to replace all their teeth with dental implants. The implants are placed at an angle, giving the patient a full arch of new teeth that look healthy and natural.

The patient receives only four implants total, two at the front and two at the back. Once these are in place, the restoration process can begin with the attachment of a full denture to the implants. With proper care and maintenance, an All-On-4 device can last for 10+ years.

## Are All-On-X Dental Implants Right for Me?

![Dentist showing a patient a denture model](assets/images/all-on-x-dental-implants-2.webp)

Some people may be candidates for full arch restorations instead of individual implants. In these cases, the number of dental implants to be placed is determined and inserted in the upper or lower arch. A fixed denture is secured to the implants to restore the function of a full arch of teeth. However, this type of procedure isn’t ideal for everyone. Most people who opt for traditional implants still receive individual crowns for their teeth. This is because there isn’t enough bone density in the jaw to support an entire arch of teeth at once. Your dentist can help you determine if you’re eligible for an All-On-X procedure or whether you need standard implants.

## How Do All-On-X Dental Implants Work?

![Man in dental chair looking at his smile in mirror](assets/images/all-on-x-dental-implants-3.webp)

The All-On-X treatment concept relies on four or six implants that support an entire arch of replacement teeth. These implants are placed at an angle to maximize support and stability, and they function like a tooth root to provide a strong foundation for artificial teeth. A customized denture is secured to the implants to restore a full smile. Dentures are crafted to look just like natural teeth and blend in with surrounding teeth.

Patients who are missing most or all of their teeth can benefit from this treatment method. If you have significant bone loss in the jaw area, you may need a bone graft prior to receiving your permanent restorations.

## What Are the Benefits of All-On-X Dental Implants?

![Young man and older man sitting on couch and talking](assets/images/all-on-x-dental-implants-4.webp)

There are many benefits to choosing an implant-supported denture design over removable prosthetics. While traditional dentures rest on the gums, this design requires at least four or six dental implants to be placed into the jawbone. These implants provide a stable foundation that supports the prosthetic to prevent movement, shifting, and slipping. Patients with this prosthesis can eat and speak comfortably without worrying about their false teeth moving out of place.

Another benefit of All-On-X dental implants in Reno is that they prevent jawbone deterioration. Traditional removable dentures can sometimes cause the jawbone to weaken because it is no longer being used to support teeth. Dentures can also break down over time and cause irritation to the gums. However, dental implants act like natural tooth roots and provide stimulation to the bone when chewing food. This prevents the deterioration of the jawbone and improves oral health.

## All-on-X FAQs

### How Long Does All-on-X Treatment Take?

The overall duration of All-on-X treatment can vary depending on the circumstances of each case. Typically, the entire process, from initial consultation to the placement of a permanent denture, can take several months.

The surgical procedure itself is relatively quick, often completed within a few hours. During this appointment, we may remove your remaining natural teeth, place your implants, and provide you with a temporary denture. After a healing period of a few months, your temporary denture should be replaced by a permanent one.

### How Much Do All-on-X Dental Implants Cost?

The cost of All-on-X dental implants can vary based on several factors, including the number of implants required, the complexity of the case, and the materials used.

It is important to note that our dental practice offers financing options to make the treatment more accessible. During your consultation, we will provide you with a personal cost estimate and help you understand your payment options. Remember that All-on-X is an excellent value!

### Does All-on-X Treatment Hurt?

We make every effort to minimize discomfort throughout your treatment process.

During the surgical procedure, we often use sedation and local anesthesia, so you can expect to feel little to nothing. Post-surgery, some level of discomfort is normal as the mouth heals and adjusts to the implants. However, this discomfort is typically manageable with pain medication. Most patients report that any discomfort is significantly less than anticipated. Our team will provide you with detailed aftercare instructions to help manage and alleviate any post-operative discomfort.

### What Is the Difference Between All-on-X and Regular Implant Dentures?

All-on-X treatment differs from regular implant dentures in several key ways:

-   Regular implant dentures often require a different number of implants. (They can require more or fewer, depending on the specifics of each case.) In contrast, All-on-X uses a strategically placed set of 4 – 6 implants to support a full arch of teeth.
-   All-on-X reduces the need for bone grafting and allows for a more efficient treatment process than most traditional implant procedures.
-   All-on-X implant dentures are permanently attached to the implants that support them. Some traditional implant dentures are removable.

### What Can I Eat After All-on-X Surgery?

In the initial days after your surgery, you should consume only soft foods and liquids, such as smoothies, soups, and mashed potatoes. Gradually, you can introduce more solid foods as healing progresses, while avoiding hard or chewy foods that may put stress on the implants. Our team will provide comprehensive dietary guidelines tailored to your unique situation. Following these recommendations will promote optimal healing and a smooth overall recovery process.

# Replace Missing Teeth Reno

## Rebuilding Smiles for Happier, Healthier Lives

Studies estimate around 180 million Americans have at least one missing tooth, and around a quarter of that group suffers from total tooth loss. However, having any number of missing teeth will put you at a pretty significant disadvantage when it comes to eating, speaking, and maintaining excellent oral health. Luckily, our team here at Smile Design Implant Centers excels at helping patients rebuild their smiles and vastly boost their overall quality of life. To learn more about our options for replacing missing teeth in Reno, contact our dental office today!

## Why Choose Smile Design Implant Centers for Replacing Missing Teeth?

-   Board-Certified Prosthodontist On-Staff
-   Pressure-Free Complimentary Consultations
-   Numerous Flexible Financing Options Available

## Dental Bridges

![Illustrated dental bridge on table next to dental mirror](assets/images/missing-teeth-1.webp)

Dental bridges do as their name implies, and “bridge” the gaps left by missing teeth. They consist of realistic replacement teeth attached to dental crowns that are secured to the teeth adjacent to the gap. In some cases, dental implants can instead be used to secure a bridge. Bridges are ideal for replacing one, two, or three teeth in a row; for more severe tooth loss, something like a partial or full denture is normally recommended.

[Learn More About Dental Bridges](dental-bridges.html)

## Dentures

![Dental professional holding a denture](assets/images/missing-teeth-2.webp)

Dentures are perhaps the most recognizable of all tooth replacement options, having assisted countless generations of patients with varying degrees of missing teeth. They consist of custom-made replacement teeth that are attached to a gum-colored base, so rest assured, they look remarkably lifelike. They’re typically held in place using a combination of adhesives and muscle control, but in some cases, dentures can be attached to dental implants for a sturdier, more reliable, long-term solution.

[Learn More About Dentures](dentures.html)

## Dental Implants

![Illustrated dental implant replacing a missing tooth](assets/images/missing-teeth-3.webp)

Dental implants are often considered the gold standard of tooth replacement, and when you consider all of the benefits that they’re able to provide for patients, it’s easy to see why they’re so incredible. They can replace the entire structure of missing teeth, restore an incredible amount of bite power, and endure for decades under the right circumstances. They can also be used to address virtually any degree of tooth loss. With dental implants, you’re truly investing in your smile’s bright future!

[Learn More About Dental Implants](dental-implants.html)

## Full Mouth Rehabilitation

![Man in dental chair admiring his smile in mirror](assets/images/missing-teeth-4.webp)

Everyone’s smile is unique – which means that everyone experiences tooth loss differently. Some people lose teeth due to unchecked gum disease or other oral health issues, while others suffer unpredictable physical trauma. No matter how severe your tooth loss is, our practice is committed to helping you find a solution. Full mouth rehabilitation is a process that can be used to comprehensively improve a smile’s health, function, and appearance all at once. During your complimentary consultation, we’ll carefully inspect your smile, discuss your preferences with you, and let you know what we think might work for your situation.

# Dentures – Reno, NV

## Rebuild and Revitalize Your Smile

Living with [missing teeth](https://www.smiledesignimplants.com/missing-teeth.html) can make everyday life a lot more difficult in ways you may not anticipate. Even showing your smile when talking to a loved one can feel uncomfortable. At Smile Design Implant Centers, we design natural-looking dentures in Reno specifically tailored to your unique bite, facial features, and lifestyle. Using advanced technology and high-quality materials, our team can replace several teeth or an entire arch so you can enjoy a confident, functional smile again. Whether you’re new to dentures or ready for an upgrade, we’re happy to help.

## Why Choose Smile Design Implant Centers For Dentures?

-   Comfortable, Customized Denture Prosthetics
-   No-Mess Digital Impression System
-   Quality Dental Materials for High Aesthetics

## Who Is a Good Candidate for Dentures?

![Reno patient smiling confidently with new dentures](assets/images/dentures-1.jpg)

Almost any adult with a generally healthy mouth and a desire to replace lost teeth can be a good candidate for dentures. The people who benefit the most often include those who:

-   **Have Speech Difficulties:** Missing teeth can affect pronunciation and clarity. Dentures help restore natural speech.
-   **Are Committed to Treatment:** Successful dentures require regular wear, good home care, and follow-up visits.
-   **Can’t Eat the Foods They Love:** Dentures improve chewing so you can enjoy more of your favorite meals again.
-   **Are Embarrassed to Smile:** A complete, natural-looking smile can boost self-esteem anywhere you go.
-   **Have Good Oral Health:** Healthy gums and remaining teeth support better fit, comfort, and long-term success.

## Types of Dentures

![ Reno patient smiling with dentures](assets/images/dentures-2.jpg)

The right denture for you depends on how many teeth you’re missing, your jawbone health, and your lifestyle. During your consultation, we’ll examine your mouth, discuss your goals, and recommend a personalized treatment plan, so you know exactly what your next steps will look like.

### Partial Dentures

![Dentist in Reno holding different styles of dentures](assets/images/dentures-3.jpg)

Partial dentures are ideal when you still have some healthy teeth alongside larger gaps that make eating and smiling difficult. They typically feature a metal or acrylic base that anchors to your natural teeth with metal loops or clasps. This design restores both function and appearance while helping prevent your other teeth from shifting. 

### Full Dentures

![Model showing partial denture replacing several missing teeth](assets/images/dentures-4.jpg)

Full dentures replace all the teeth in your upper or lower arch. They are crafted to rest comfortably on your gums, often relying on natural suction, muscle control, and a precise fit to stay in place. This model uses a custom-fit base with replacement teeth positioned to support your lips and cheeks, giving you a fuller, more youthful appearance.

### Implant Dentures

![Dentist holding a full upper denture](assets/images/dentures-5.jpg)

If you’re looking for a more permanent and stable solution for tooth loss, implant dentures are an excellent choice. These prosthetics attach to [dental implants](https://www.smiledesignimplants.com/dental-implants.html) placed into your jawbone. Once attached, these anchors prevent our dentures from slipping, shifting, or rubbing your gums. If you want the very best in stability, comfort, and functionality, implant dentures might be for you.

## The Benefits of Dentures

![Denture model attached to dental implants in jaw](assets/images/dentures-6.jpg)

Dentures do more than just “fill the gaps.” In fact, the right prosthetic can significantly improve your quality of life. With a well-made denture from Smile Design Implant Centers, you can enjoy:

-   **Improved Chewing Ability:** Eat a wider variety of foods more comfortably.
-   **Clearer Speech:** Replace missing teeth that make certain sounds difficult to pronounce.
-   **Enhanced Confidence:** Smile, laugh, and socialize without worrying about tooth gaps.
-   **Facial Support:** Keep your face looking full and youthful.
-   **Protection for Remaining Teeth:** Partial dentures help distribute biting forces more evenly.

With dentures in Reno, revitalizing your smile can be a life-changing decision, and we’re proud to be a part of that transformation. [Contact us today](https://www.smiledesignimplants.com/contact.html), and we’ll set up a consultation.
